1. g1h1: The King moves to h1. This is a safe move.
2. f1f2: The Rook blocks the check. But the Bishop on c5 can just capture the Rook (c5xf2). This loses a Rook.
3. d1d4: The Queen blocks the check. But the Bishop on c5 can capture the Queen (c5xd4), OR the Knight on c6 can capture the Queen (c6xd4), OR the Queen on e6 can capture the Queen (e6xd4). This loses the Queen.

- d1d4: Loses queen to c5xd4, c6xd4, or e6xd4.
- g1h1: King moves to safety.
